## Goal5486-5488 LibRTS exact point-contains phase work (2026-07-11/12)

Goal5486 completed a six-case exact official-input prepared-phase matrix. All
six author/RTDL result counts matched on the same extracted geometry/query
files. Load, index preparation, prepared query wall, and primitive query time
remain separate; no performance ratio or Figure 6 claim is authorized.

Goal5487 added the generic public `Aabb2DColumns` contract and
`prepare_aabb_index_2d_columns` front door. The POD tiny gate matched the old
row front door and reported RT-core acceleration. This is a host ABI/packing
improvement, not device zero-copy.

Goal5488 changed the LibRTS app-owned WKT front door to emit the generic column
contract and ran exact `dtl_cnty` and `lakes.bz2` gates. Counts and exact input
hashes matched. On the lakes case the old prepared-index phase was about
66.3s and the columnar phase about 0.856s, while WKT parsing stayed about
405s. The first columnar query was not a controlled comparison, so no speedup
claim is made.

## Goal5489 LibRTS same-process prepared-query repeat (2026-07-12)

Goal5489 adds an app-owned repeat runner that prepares one generic columnar
AABB index and executes the same exact point-query batch at least twice in one
process. It records first-query versus subsequent-query wall and primitive
times, checks every repeated count against the author count, and forbids
performance ratios, device-zero-copy claims, Figure 6 claims, and Embree.
POD evidence now covers `dtl_cnty` and `lakes.bz2`, with all three repeats
matching each author count. The WKT load is `27.994s` and `406.570s`
respectively; the prepared-query wall settles near `0.219s` and `0.221s`.
This is a regime diagnostic, not a performance headline. A NumPy numeric WKT
loader variant is prepared as the next app-owned experiment.

## Goal5490 LibRTS numeric WKT loader probe (2026-07-12)

Goal5490 added an app-owned NumPy numeric WKT loader variant and verified local
column parity. On exact `dtl_cnty`, all three counts matched `136475`, but the
separate-run load was `28.069s` versus the Goal5489 regex run's `27.994s`.
No material load improvement was demonstrated, so this is a no-go for a
speedup and the variant remains experimental. WKT parsing stays outside RTDL
core.

## Goal5491 LibRTS exact AABB column cache (2026-07-12)

Goal5491 adds an app-owned atomic `.npz`/JSON cache for exact WKT-derived AABB
columns. The metadata binds source size, SHA-256, row count, dtype, and schema;
load rejects stale source hashes. On exact `lakes.bz2`, the 286MB cache loads
with source-hash validation in `8.101s`, prepares in `0.840s`, and three
queries match author count `103189` with prepared-query wall near `0.218s`.
The original WKT parse was `406.570s`; cache build is separate, so this is
reusable-ingestion evidence, not an end-to-end speedup claim.

## External review of Goals5485-5491 (2026-07-12)

The batch review approved Goals5485-5491, with Goal5490 explicitly approved as
a no-go. The review confirmed `Aabb2DColumns` genericity and non-app use,
phase-boundary honesty, cache atomic publication and four-way fail-closed
validation, and hash-bound author evidence reuse. No performance ratio,
end-to-end speedup, pointwise relation, Figure 6, full-paper, device-zero-copy,
or Embree claim was authorized.

## Goal5480-5481 LibRTS exact point-contains gate (2026-07-11)

The official 23,062,425,365-byte archive was already MD5 verified and safely
inventoried. Full extraction failed closed at 72GB because the POD has a hidden
quota despite the shared filesystem reporting 357TB free. Goal5480 adds
evidence reuse, resumable staging, and atomic selected-member extraction. It
extracts exact `dtl_cnty.wkt` plus its 100K point-query file and records their
SHA-256 hashes without claiming the whole archive is expanded.

Goal5481 runs author RTSpatial and the generic RTDL OptiX
`expanded_aabb_point_membership_rows_2d` route on those identical files. The
author and RTDL both report 136,475 rows (`matched=true`); RTDL metadata reports
RT-core acceleration and no native engine customization. Author pair rows are
not exposed, Figure 6 is not reproduced, and no performance ratio is allowed.

## Goals5492-5495 LibRTS exact-operation closeout (2026-07-12)

Goal5492 scanned the verified official archive without full extraction and
found `14` exact point-contains pairs, `14` exact range-contains pairs, and
`42` exact range-intersects pairs. It found no exact PIP or mutation pairs;
those operations remain blocked for this archive rather than using substitutes.

Goal5493 selected the exact `dtl_cnty` range-contains geometry/query members,
recorded their SHA-256 values, and ran the pinned author binary plus the
generic `Aabb2DColumns -> prepare_aabb_index_2d_columns -> count` route on
the same files. Both returned count `117314`. RTDL load, prepare, query wall,
and primitive time were kept separate; no author ratio was computed.

Goal5494 decided that the Goal5491 hash-bound `.npz`/JSON cache remains
app-owned. It has no second non-LibRTS consumer and encodes WKT/provenance
policy, so no RTDL cache lifecycle API is promoted.

Goal5495 closes the current exact point-contains/range-contains AABB line and
queues exact range-intersects as the next operation. Goals5492-5495 are
implemented and documented but remain external-review pending. No Figure 6,
full-paper, pointwise relation, performance-parity, device-zero-copy, or
Embree claim is authorized.

## Goal5496 LibRTS exact range-intersects count gate (2026-07-12)

Goal5496 selected the exact `dtl_cnty` range-intersects member from the
verified archive and reused the already verified geometry member from Goal5493.
The query SHA-256 is `f901553c4d9a25f1df567051f5a1f940723d46a7c74e25a02339eb25f185dce7`.
Author and RTDL both returned count `1,570,285` for `12,234` geometries and
`10,000` queries. The author gate used `load_factor=1` after an otherwise
identical `0.0001` run failed with CUDA invalid program counter; this choice
is explicit in the result artifact. RTDL phases were load `27.842s`, prepare
`0.446s`, prepared query wall `0.660s`, and primitive `0.627s`.

Goal5496 is implemented and external-review pending. It is exact-input
count-level evidence only; no pointwise relation, Figure 6, full-paper,
performance-ratio, device-zero-copy, or Embree claim is authorized.

## Goal5501 LibRTS Bounded Project Closeout (2026-07-12)

Goal5501 completed the final diagnostic required after the Goal5500 exact

## Goal5502 LibRTS Author-Validity Gate (2026-07-12)

Goal5502 formalizes the owner decision rule without changing RTDL. An
app-owned gate compares five Goal5501 same-source prefixes against an
independent inclusive float32 AABB contract. RTDL matches the selected
contract on all five; the author matches on one and diverges on four. The gate
therefore selects `preserve_generic_rtdl` for the current evidence and
authorizes no LibRTS-specific core behavior.

This is prefix-only evidence. It does not prove the author is wrong on the
full inputs, and it does not prove full-input author validity. If future
full-input evidence instead shows author/oracle agreement and RTDL divergence,
RTDL must be fixed before an exact reproduction claim. The artifact and tests
are recorded under `results/goal5502/` and `tests/goal5502_*`.

## Goal5503 LibRTS author GPU contract audit (2026-07-12)

Goal5503 is implemented and review pending. The pinned POD source audit now
covers the actual range-intersects OptiX path, not only the CPU envelope helper.
The benchmark uses float32 coordinates and direct float-to-OptixAabb conversion.
The GPU shader uses `RayParams<float,2>::IsHit` with `t0=0`,
`nextafterf(1.0, FLT_MAX)`, `tFar *= 1 + 2 * FLT_GAMMA(3)`, and a forward
query diagonal followed by a reverse envelope diagonal. The CPU inclusive
oracle and this GPU predicate are intentionally recorded as distinct; their
equivalence is not proven. No RTDL core change is authorized. Goal5504 must
use discriminating fixtures before any full-input campaign.

Evidence:

```text
Paper-reproduction-apps/librts-paper/results/goal5503_author_contract_audit.json
history/internal_docs/goal5503_librts_author_range_intersects_contract_result_2026-07-12.md
```

The focused and 5492-5503 LibRTS regression suite passes: 26 tests OK.

## Goal5508 Generic float32-degenerate indexed AABB validity fix (2026-07-12)

Goal5508 resolves both remaining Goal5502 official-prefix count differences.
The author and RTDL parsers have identical float32 MBR fingerprints. Each
prefix contains four indexed geometries that become zero-width or zero-height
after float32 conversion. A four-record-only probe reproduced the full excess:
pre-fix RTDL returned `27` for parks and `5,005` for lakes, while the author
returned zero. The generic OptiX intersection kernel now checks strict indexed
AABB validity using the correct indexed record in both forward and backward
passes. Fixed clean-POD counts match author exactly:

```text
parks_Europe: 34,240,217 == 34,240,217
lakes_bz2:    34,581,812 == 34,581,812
```

This is a generic native correction, not a complete official archive matrix,
pair-row proof, performance ratio, or full-paper reproduction. Goal5508 is
implemented and local tests pass; external review remains pending.
